Title: Memoirs of the Life of Mrs. Sarah Osborn, who died at Newport, Rhode Island, on the second day of August 1796, in the eighty-third year of her age; by Samuel Hopkins, D.D., Pastor of the First Congregational Church in Newport
Creator: Sarah Osborn (1714-1796), her diaries and memoir; compiled by Samuel Hopkins (1721-1803)
Publisher: Worcester, Massachusetts: printed by Leonard Worcester
Publication Date: 1799
Rights: Public Domain (published 1799, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item memoirsoflifeofm1799hopk (https://archive.org/details/memoirsoflifeofm1799hopk), the item's own memoirsoflifeofm1799hopk_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Boston Public Library; host metadata date: 1799. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 54c0e2bb97d9891dcdae89dc52b0d539a124f029ffc7abbfed84f86a54b28c98.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Boston Public Library scan; clean OCR after a garbled title page. Hopkins quotes Osborn's diaries and her memoir of 1743 at length.
Content note: Sarah Osborn's spiritual diaries and her own memoir of her early life, as Hopkins extracted them: a Newport schoolmistress's forty years of recorded experience, including the religious meetings of enslaved people in her house in the 1760s; the census-named women's voice, in the only public-domain printing.
